mysql> CREATE TABLE Accounts (
    ->     account_id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    ->     customer_id INT NOT NULL,
    ->     branch_id INT NOT NULL,
    ->     account_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    ->     balance DECIMAL(12,2) DEFAULT 0.00,
    ->     opening_date DATE,
    ->     status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Active',
    ->
    ->     FOREIGN KEY (customer_id)
    ->         REFERENCES Customers(customer_id),
    ->
    ->     FOREIGN KEY (branch_id)
    ->         REFERENCES Branches(branch_id)
    -> );

mysql> SELECT 'Customers' AS table_name, COUNT(*) AS record_count
    -> FROM Customers
    ->
    -> UNION ALL
    ->
    -> SELECT 'Branches', COUNT(*)
    -> FROM Branches
    ->
    -> UNION ALL
    ->
    -> SELECT 'Accounts', COUNT(*)
    -> FROM Accounts
    ->
    -> UNION ALL
    ->
    -> SELECT 'Employees', COUNT(*)
    -> FROM Employees
    ->
    -> UNION ALL
    ->
    -> SELECT 'Loans', COUNT(*)
    -> FROM Loans
    ->
    -> UNION ALL
    ->
    -> SELECT 'Transactions', COUNT(*)
    -> FROM Transactions;
+--------------+--------------+
| table_name   | record_count |
+--------------+--------------+
| Customers    |           50 |
| Branches     |           10 |
| Accounts     |           70 |
| Employees    |           30 |

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     COALESCE(
    ->         SUM(
    ->             CASE
    ->                 WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Deposit'
    ->                     THEN t.amount
    ->                 WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Withdrawal'
    ->                     THEN -t.amount
    ->                 ELSE 0
    ->             END
    ->         ),
    ->         0
    ->     ) AS net_transaction_amount

    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id

    ->     a.account_id,

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     a.customer_id,
    ->     c.registration_date,
    ->     a.opening_date,
    ->     MIN(t.transaction_date) AS first_transaction_date
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> WHERE a.opening_date < c.registration_date
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     a.customer_id,
    ->     c.registration_date,
    ->     a.opening_date
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     a.customer_id,
    ->     a.account_id;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     c.registration_date,
    ->     MIN(a.opening_date) AS first_account_date,
    ->     MIN(t.transaction_date) AS first_transaction_date
    -> FROM Customers c
    -> JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    -> LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     c.registration_date
    -> HAVING c.registration_date > MIN(a.opening_date)
    -> ORDER BY c.customer_id;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     c.registration_date AS old_registration_date,
    ->     MIN(a.opening_date) AS new_registration_date

    -> JOIN Accounts a

mysql> UPDATE Customers c
    -> JOIN (
    ->     SELECT
    ->         customer_id,
    ->         MIN(opening_date) AS first_account_date
    ->     FROM Accounts
    ->     GROUP BY customer_id
    -> ) a
    ->     ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    -> SET c.registration_date = a.first_account_date
    -> WHERE c.registration_date > a.first_account_date;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     l.loan_id,
    ->     l.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     MIN(a.opening_date) AS first_account_date,
    ->     l.loan_date,
    ->     l.loan_amount
    -> FROM Loans l
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON l.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON l.customer_id = a.customer_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     l.loan_id,
    ->     l.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     l.loan_date,
    ->     l.loan_amount
    -> HAVING l.loan_date < MIN(a.opening_date)
    -> ORDER BY l.customer_id, l.loan_date;

    ->     loan_id,

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     a.customer_id,
    ->     a.account_type,
    ->     COUNT(t.transaction_id) AS transaction_count,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(t.amount), 0) AS total_transaction_amount
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     a.customer_id,
    ->     a.account_type
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     a.account_id;
+------------+-------------+--------------+-------------------+--------------------------+
| account_id | customer_id | account_type | transaction_count | total_transaction_amount |
+------------+-------------+--------------+-------------------+--------------------------+
|          1 |           1 | Savings      |                 5 |                 87500.00 |
|          2 |           2 | Current      |                 5 |                149000.00 |
|          3 |           3 | Savings      |                 5 |                 77000.00 |

mysql> SELECT
    ->     transaction_type,
    ->     COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
    ->     SUM(amount) AS total_amount,
    ->     AVG(amount) AS average_amount,
    ->     MIN(amount) AS minimum_amount,
    ->     MAX(amount) AS maximum_amount
    -> FROM Transactions
    -> GROUP BY transaction_type
    -> ORDER BY total_amount DESC;
+------------------+-------------------+--------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| transaction_type | transaction_count | total_amount | average_amount | minimum_amount | maximum_amount |

| Withdrawal       |               120 |   1070500.00 |    8920.833333 |        3000.00 |       18000.00 |

mysql> SELECT
    ->     DATE_FORMAT(transaction_date, '%Y-%m') AS transaction_month,
    ->     COUNT(*) AS transaction_count,
    ->     SUM(amount) AS total_transaction_amount,
    ->     AVG(amount) AS average_transaction_amount
    -> FROM Transactions
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     DATE_FORMAT(transaction_date, '%Y-%m')
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     transaction_month;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name,
    ->     b.location,
    ->     COUNT(a.account_id) AS account_count,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(a.balance), 0) AS total_balance
    -> FROM Branches b
    -> LEFT JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name,
    ->     b.location
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     total_balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name,
    ->     COUNT(t.transaction_id) AS transaction_count,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(t.amount), 0) AS total_transaction_amount
    -> FROM Branches b
    -> LEFT JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id
    -> LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     transaction_count DESC,
    ->     total_transaction_amount DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Deposit'
    ->         THEN t.amount
    ->         ELSE 0
    ->     END), 0) AS total_deposits,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Withdrawal'
    ->         THEN t.amount
    ->         ELSE 0
    ->     END), 0) AS total_withdrawals,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Deposit'
    ->         THEN t.amount
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Withdrawal'
    ->         THEN -t.amount
    ->         ELSE 0
    ->     END), 0) AS net_transaction_amount
    -> FROM Branches b
    -> LEFT JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id
    -> LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     net_transaction_amount DESC;
+-----------+-----------------------+----------------+-------------------+------------------------+
| branch_id | branch_name           | total_deposits | total_withdrawals | net_transaction_amount |
+-----------+-----------------------+----------------+-------------------+------------------------+
|         5 | Madhapur Branch       |     1442000.00 |         197500.00 |             1244500.00 |
|        10 | Visakhapatnam Branch  |     1311000.00 |         168000.00 |             1143000.00 |

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     COUNT(DISTINCT a.account_id) AS account_count,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(DISTINCT a.balance), 0) AS total_balance,
    ->     COUNT(t.transaction_id) AS transaction_count,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Deposit'
    ->         THEN t.amount
    ->         ELSE 0
    ->     END), 0) AS total_deposits,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Withdrawal'
    ->         THEN t.amount
    ->         ELSE 0
    ->     END), 0) AS total_withdrawals
    -> FROM Customers c
    -> LEFT JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    -> LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     total_balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     COALESCE(a.account_count, 0) AS account_count,
    ->     COALESCE(a.total_balance, 0) AS total_balance,
    ->     COALESCE(t.transaction_count, 0) AS transaction_count,
    ->     COALESCE(t.total_deposits, 0) AS total_deposits,
    ->     COALESCE(t.total_withdrawals, 0) AS total_withdrawals
    -> FROM Customers c
    ->
    -> LEFT JOIN (
    ->     SELECT
    ->         customer_id,
    ->         COUNT(*) AS account_count,
    ->         SUM(balance) AS total_balance
    ->     FROM Accounts
    ->     GROUP BY customer_id
    -> ) a
    ->     ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    ->
    -> LEFT JOIN (
    ->     SELECT
    ->         a.customer_id,
    ->         COUNT(t.transaction_id) AS transaction_count,
    ->         SUM(CASE
    ->             WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Deposit'
    ->             THEN t.amount
    ->             ELSE 0
    ->         END) AS total_deposits,
    ->         SUM(CASE
    ->             WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Withdrawal'
    ->             THEN t.amount
    ->             ELSE 0
    ->         END) AS total_withdrawals
    ->     FROM Accounts a
    ->     LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->         ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    ->     GROUP BY a.customer_id
    -> ) t
    ->     ON c.customer_id = t.customer_id
    ->
    -> ORDER BY total_balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Deposit'
    ->         THEN t.amount
    ->         WHEN t.transaction_type = 'Withdrawal'
    ->         THEN -t.amount
    ->         ELSE 0
    ->     END), 0) AS net_transaction_amount
    -> FROM Customers c
    -> LEFT JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    -> LEFT JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name
    -> ORDER BY
    ->     net_transaction_amount DESC;

mysql> WITH customer_balances AS (
    ->     SELECT
    ->         c.customer_id,
    ->         c.name,
    ->         SUM(a.balance) AS total_balance
    ->     FROM Customers c
    ->     JOIN Accounts a
    ->         ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    ->     GROUP BY
    ->         c.customer_id,
    ->         c.name
    -> )
    -> SELECT
    ->     customer_id,
    ->     name,
    ->     total_balance,
    ->     RANK() OVER (
    ->         ORDER BY total_balance DESC
    ->     ) AS balance_rank
    -> FROM customer_balances
    -> ORDER BY balance_rank;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.account_type,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     RANK() OVER (
    ->         PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) AS type_rank
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> ORDER BY a.account_type, type_rank;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.account_type,
    ->     a.balance
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> WHERE a.account_id IN (
    ->     SELECT account_id
    ->     FROM (
    ->         SELECT
    ->             account_id,
    ->             RANK() OVER (
    ->                 PARTITION BY account_type
    ->                 ORDER BY balance DESC
    ->             ) AS rnk
    ->         FROM Accounts
    ->     ) ranked
    ->     WHERE rnk = 1
    -> )
    -> ORDER BY a.account_type;
+------------+-----------------+--------------+-----------+
| account_id | name            | account_type | balance   |
+------------+-----------------+--------------+-----------+
|         32 | Gautham Kumar   | Current      | 153000.00 |
|         42 | Yashwanth Kumar | Salary       |  99000.00 |
|         25 | Sravani Patel   | Savings      | 245000.00 |
+------------+-----------------+--------------+-----------+

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     LAG(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) AS previous_balance,
    ->     LAG(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) - a.balance AS balance_difference
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> ORDER BY a.balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     LEAD(a.balance) OVER (

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     LEAD(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) AS next_balance,
    ->     a.balance - LEAD(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) AS balance_difference
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> ORDER BY a.balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.account_type,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     FIRST_VALUE(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) AS highest_balance
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> ORDER BY a.account_type, a.balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.account_type,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     FIRST_VALUE(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) AS highest_balance,
    ->     FIRST_VALUE(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->     ) - a.balance AS difference_from_highest
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> ORDER BY a.account_type, a.balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.account_type,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     LAST_VALUE(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->         ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->         ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING
    ->     ) AS lowest_balance
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> ORDER BY a.account_type, a.balance DESC;

mysql> WITH ranked_accounts AS (
    ->     SELECT
    ->         a.account_id,
    ->         c.name,
    ->         a.account_type,
    ->         a.balance,
    ->         RANK() OVER (
    ->             PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->             ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->         ) AS balance_rank
    ->     FROM Accounts a
    ->     JOIN Customers c
    ->         ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> )
    -> SELECT
    ->     account_id,
    ->     name,
    ->     account_type,
    ->     balance,
    ->     balance_rank
    -> FROM ranked_accounts
    -> WHERE balance_rank <= 3
    -> ORDER BY account_type, balance_rank;
+------------+-----------------+--------------+-----------+--------------+
| account_id | name            | account_type | balance   | balance_rank |
+------------+-----------------+--------------+-----------+--------------+
|         32 | Gautham Kumar   | Current      | 153000.00 |            1 |

mysql> WITH ranked_accounts AS (
    ->     SELECT
    ->         a.account_id,
    ->         c.name,
    ->         a.account_type,
    ->         a.balance,
    ->         ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
    ->             PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->             ORDER BY a.balance DESC
    ->         ) AS row_num
    ->     FROM Accounts a
    ->     JOIN Customers c
    ->         ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> )
    -> SELECT
    ->     account_id,
    ->     name,
    ->     account_type,
    ->     balance,
    ->     row_num
    -> FROM ranked_accounts
    -> WHERE row_num <= 3
    -> ORDER BY account_type, row_num;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     a.account_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     a.account_type,
    ->     a.balance,
    ->     SUM(a.balance) OVER (
    ->         PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->     ) AS type_total_balance,
    ->     ROUND(
    ->         (a.balance / SUM(a.balance) OVER (
    ->             PARTITION BY a.account_type
    ->         )) * 100,
    ->         2
    ->     ) AS balance_percentage
    -> FROM Accounts a
    -> JOIN Customers c
    ->     ON a.customer_id = c.customer_id
    -> ORDER BY a.account_type, a.balance DESC;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     SUM(a.balance) AS total_balance
    -> FROM Customers c
    -> JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name
    -> ORDER BY total_balance DESC
    -> LIMIT 5;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     COUNT(t.transaction_id) AS total_transactions
    -> FROM Customers c
    -> JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id
    -> JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id

    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name
    -> ORDER BY total_transactions DESC

mysql> SELECT
    ->     YEAR(transaction_date) AS transaction_year,
    ->     MONTH(transaction_date) AS transaction_month,
    ->     COUNT(transaction_id) AS total_transactions,

    -> GROUP BY
    ->     YEAR(transaction_date),
    ->     MONTH(transaction_date)

    ->     transaction_year,

mysql> SELECT
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name,
    ->     SUM(t.amount) AS total_deposits
    -> FROM Branches b
    -> JOIN Accounts a
    ->     ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id
    -> JOIN Transactions t
    ->     ON a.account_id = t.account_id
    -> WHERE t.transaction_type = 'Deposit'
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     b.branch_id,
    ->     b.branch_name
    -> ORDER BY total_deposits DESC
    -> LIMIT 1;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name,
    ->     SUM(l.loan_amount) AS total_loan_amount
    -> FROM Customers c
    -> JOIN Loans l
    ->     ON c.customer_id = l.customer_id
    -> GROUP BY
    ->     c.customer_id,
    ->     c.name
    -> ORDER BY total_loan_amount DESC

mysql> DELIMITER //
mysql>
mysql> CREATE PROCEDURE InsertNewCustomer(
    ->     IN p_customer_id INT,
    ->     IN p_name VARCHAR(100),
    ->     IN p_email VARCHAR(100),
    ->     IN p_phone VARCHAR(15),
    ->     IN p_address VARCHAR(255),
    ->     IN p_registration_date DATE
    -> )
    -> BEGIN
    ->     INSERT INTO Customers
    ->     (customer_id, name, email, phone, address, registration_date)
    ->     VALUES
    ->     (p_customer_id, p_name, p_email, p_phone, p_address, p_registration_date);
    -> END //
Query OK, 0 rows affected (0.04 sec)

mysql>
mysql> DELIMITER ;
mysql> CALL InsertNewCustomer(
    ->     51,
    ->     'Test Customer',
    ->     'testcustomer@gmail.com',
    ->     '9876543210',
    ->     'Hyderabad',
    ->     '2026-09-30'
    -> );
Query OK, 1 row affected (0.01 sec)

mysql> DELIMITER //
mysql>
mysql> CREATE PROCEDURE UpdateAccountBalance(
    ->     IN p_account_id INT,
    ->     IN p_new_balance DECIMAL(12,2)
    -> )
    -> BEGIN
    ->     UPDATE Accounts
    ->     SET balance = p_new_balance
    ->     WHERE account_id = p_account_id;
    -> END //
Query OK, 0 rows affected (0.01 sec)

mysql>

mysql> CREATE PROCEDURE DisplayAccountTransactions(
    ->     IN p_account_id INT
    -> )
    -> BEGIN
    ->     SELECT
    ->         transaction_id,
    ->         account_id,
    ->         transaction_type,
    ->         amount,
    ->         transaction_date
    ->     FROM Transactions
    ->     WHERE account_id = p_account_id
    ->     ORDER BY transaction_date;
    -> END //
